# IMG subframe audit

Generated by `python tools/img_subframe_audit.py --md`. For every IMG
library a live LOD reads, this is the set of records inside it that no
live LOD ever names - LOAD2 resolves `---> NAME` against the record
names in the library, so it is an exact difference, not an estimate.

`~bytes` is **uncompressed** `w*h*ppp/8` at the LOD's colour depth - an
upper bound. What a frame actually costs in video ROM is whatever it
packs to after LOAD2's zero compression.

| bucket | meaning |
|---|---|
| `MISSING` | a live LOD names a record the library does not carry. LOAD2 logs `Couldn't find image` and the label builds EMPTY. A build bug. |
| `UNUSED` | nothing related is loaded from any library - real unloaded art. |
| `UNCUT` | whole-frame record whose own A/B/C strips are loaded. Same art, other cut. |
| `STRIP` | strip whose uncut parent or a sibling strip is loaded. |
| `ELSEWHERE` | this exact name is loaded from a different library. |

## Totals

| | records | ~bytes |
|---|---:|---:|
| in live libraries | 15,828 | |
| loaded by a live LOD | 10,009 | |
| `UNUSED` | 1,290 | 7,258,696 |
| `UNCUT` | 4,023 | 25,799,477 |
| `STRIP` | 131 | 262,199 |
| `ELSEWHERE` | 348 | 811,642 |
| `MISSING` | 22 | |

44 live LODs, 174 IMG libraries.
